# Formulas recuperadas por el ejecutor
 simbolico (RXRE-SWF-049)

## Metodo

Ejecutor simbolico lineal (`tools/avm1_sym.py`)
sobre el stream principal AVM1. Seguimiento
de pila de expresiones simbolicas con
profundidad de expresion (`expr_depth`) para
calcular el marco de `CallMethod` (0x52):
marco = profundidad_actual - expr_depth =
args + objeto + metodo, donde expr_depth =
profundidad tras la ultima accion
terminadora (SetMember/SetVariable/Pop/
StoreRegister/CallMethod/BranchIf/Jump).
Esto recupera el # de args aunque los args
sean computados via GetMember (que reinicia
el contador de pushes consecutivos).

## Limitaciones (instrumento de investigacion)

- Ejecucion LINEAL: los merges del CFG no
  se unen (una variable puede tener el valor
  de una sola rama).
- Las llamadas a rutinas nativas (0x52)
  producen un resultado opaco `call()`; el
  argumento de retorno es desconocido.
- El marco de CallMethod es un heuristico
  (puede fallar en llamadas encadenadas).
- La MITIGACION de armadura (danos =
  f(myDamage, myArmor)) NO aparece: esta
  en la rutina nativa (token, RXRE-SWF-048)
  con firma inDamage/inArmor.

Escrituras recuperadas: 8675
con expresion aritmetica: 599

| 1632 | r1 | myDescription | ` In order to bring about the destruction of the Nexus Project and the Nexus Training Program, Hank has been hired to enlist the help of incarcerated agents Sanford and Deimos.` |
| 1632 | r1 | myDifficulty | `4` |

| 1632 | r1 | myName | `[1-B] Armory` |
| 1632 | r1 | myDescription | ` We're in danger of Nexus Units advancing their equipment loadouts. Hank is required elsewhere, so Sanford and Deimos will have to infiltrate the Armory and put an end to the Nexus weapon stockpile.` |
| 1632 | r1 | myDifficulty | `1` |

| 1632 | r1 | myName | `[1-C] Com-Tower` |
| 1632 | r1 | myDescription | ` Opposition is getting stiff. The probability of hitting the Headquarters of Project Nexus with any reliability will be nil unless we can disrupt its communications array.` |
| 1632 | r1 | myDifficulty | `2` |
| 1632 | var.MadnessLevel | totalRooms | `10` |
| 1632 | 3 | 1 | `10` |
| 1632 | r1 | myName | `[1-D] Barracks` |
| 1632 | r1 | myDescription | ` With Nexus Units reeling from the satelite blackout, we may now launch a stealth strike directly against their most precious and plentiful resource: their manpower. Kill them all!` |
| 1632 | var.MadnessLevel | totalRooms | `14` |
| 1632 | 2 | 1 | `14` |
| 1632 | r1 | myName | `[1-E] Recon Hut` |
| 1632 | r1 | myDescription | ` With the Communication Tower down and the Barracks totally obliterated, the Nexus has been forced to adapt. Engineers have been dispatched to convert a nearby Recon Hut into a new Com-Tower. Disable the facility.` |
| 1632 | r1 | myDifficulty | `3` |
| 1632 | r1 | myName | `[1-F] Stronghold` |
| 1632 | r1 | myDescription | ` Intercepted transmissions report the deployment of new G03LM Mk1 units for training at the Nexus Stronghold. Infiltrate the facility, learn what you can, and elminate this new threat.` |
| 1632 | var.MadnessLevel | totalRooms | `15` |

| 1632 | r1 | myName | `[1-G] Bar` |
| 1632 | r1 | myDescription | ` It's time to prepare for the final assault on the Nexus Headquarters. A nearby bar is operating as a front for a munitions depot, and hitting this location will benefit us twofold: we'll be well-equipped for the final battle, and Nexus morale will plummet.` |
